# Test subsystem table

This file combines the test subsystem tables for density, surface tension, viscosity, and electrical conductivity. Test-record metrics are reported as RMSE/MAE/r, with Pearson r = cor(target, prediction).

## Density

Model: CW+RK model N=0. Test-record metrics are reported in the order RMSE/MAE/r, using Pearson r = cor(target, prediction). Each test system is listed as a vertical block of rows: target system followed by available density subsystem records by record_id, mole ratio, and temperature interval. Missing records are marked with `-`.

## Surface tension

Model: Linear model. Test-record metrics are reported in the order RMSE/MAE/r, using Pearson r = cor(target, prediction). Each test system is listed as a vertical block of rows: target system followed by available surface tension subsystem records by record_id, mole ratio, and temperature interval. Missing records are marked with `-`.

Each test system is listed as a vertical block of rows: target system followed by available electrical conductivity subsystem records by record_id, mole ratio, and temperature interval. Missing records are marked with `-`.
